Projection onto simplex {x≥0, sum=1}: standard O(n log n) by sorting or bisection. For n=6733, bisection 50 iterations O(336k) per step; sorting O(n log n) maybe faster in numpy? We can implement sort-based projection:
def project_simplex(v):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.where(u * (np.arange(1,n+1)) + 1 > cssv).argmax()+1? Standard.
But bisection easy. Sorting in numpy C fast. Use sort method:
def project_simplex(a):
    n=len(a)
    u=np.sort(a)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) + 1 > cssv
    rho = int(np.argmax(cond)) + 1
    theta = (cssv[rho-1] - 1) / rho
    return np.maximum(a - theta, 0)
This projects onto sum=1. For sum S, similar: theta=(cssv[rho-1]-S)/rho; x = max(a-theta,0). This requires sorting each iteration O(n log n) in C, okay. For n=6733, 5000 iterations sort ~5000*6733log ~500M comparisons maybe okay in C within seconds? maybe 10s. Bisection pure numpy O(60n) ~400M element ops, also okay. Sort may be faster. Algorithm:
v = init (sum 1, positive)
for beta in [0.5,1,2,...]:
  for iters:
    C=conv(v,v)
    p=softmax(beta*C)
    grad = v + cross_corr(p,v)
    # grad positive? v positive, corr positive, so grad>0.
    eta = ? maybe scale = n / (np.max(grad)/np.mean(grad))? Use eta = 1.0 / np.mean(grad) * step? Since mean grad =? mean grad = mean v + mean corr. mean corr = Σ_m p_m Σ_k? For each m, Σ_k v_{m-k} ≈1, weighted =1. mean grad ≈1/n +1 =1? Wait grad_k = v_k + Σ_m p_m v_{m-k}; second term sum over k = Σ_m p_m Σ_k v_{m-k} = Σ_m p_m *1 =1. So sum grad = sum v +1 =2. Thus mean grad=2/n. So grad magnitude ~2/n.
    Choose eta = n (so eta*grad mean=2). Exponents mean 2. Maybe too large. Use eta = alpha * n, alpha 0.1-0.5.
    log_w = np.log(v) - eta * grad  (v positive; handle zeros)
    w = exp(log_w - max)
    v = w / sum(w)
This is mirror descent. It will keep positive and sum 1. No projection bisection needed; faster. We can also clip to avoid underflow.

Let's sketch mirror_anneal(v, n, iter_budget, T_schedule, beta_schedule):
for beta, T in zip(...):
  for iter in range(k):
    C = conv_fft(v)
    D = C.max()
    # compute p. Use beta scaled: p = softmax(beta*C)
    # But if beta large and T small, okay.
    maxC = C.max()
    p = np.exp(beta*(C - maxC)); p /= p.sum()
    K = cross_corr(p, v)
    grad = v + K
    gmean = grad.mean()
    if gmean ==0: break
    z = -T * (grad / gmean)
    # optionally subtract mean z to keep average? constant cancels but helps stability
    z = z - z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    v_new = v * np.exp(z)
    v_new /= v_new.sum()
    # evaluate exact Dnew
    C_new = conv_fft(v_new)

def cross_corr(p,a): ...

def soft_max_objective_w(w, n, beta):
   v=softmax(w)
   C=conv_fft(v)
   C=np.maximum(C,0)
   bmax=C.max()
   z=beta*(C-bmax)
   s=np.sum(np.exp(z))
   f = 2*n * bmax / beta? Actually if we minimize logsumexp, scaling not needed. But for gradient, soft = (1/beta) log s. Gradient wrt v: p = exp(z)/s, grad_v = v + cross_corr(p,v). Then grad_w = grad_v - v*sum(v*grad_v). Return value maybe soft (or scaled) and grad_w.
   fval = float((1.0/beta)*np.log(s))  # approximates max C
   # For final objective scale 2n, but constant.
   return fval, grad_w

Let's implement `optimize_mirror(v, n, deadline, rng, verbose=True)`:
- normalize v; C=conv; bestD=max; best=v; no_improve=0
- phase_defs: list of (tau, T_start, T_end, max_iters)
For each phase until deadline:
  beta=tau*n
  T0,T1
  iters_done=0
  while time < deadline and iters_done < max_iters:
    if C is None compute.
    p=softmax(beta*C)
    K=cross_corr(p,v)
    D=v+K
    dmean = D.mean()
    if not finite or dmean<=0: break
    frac = iters_done/max(max_iters,1)
    T = T0*(T1/T0)**frac
    z = -T*(D/dmean - 1.0)
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)
    w = v * np.exp(z)
    # If underflow all zero, fallback
    sw = w.sum()
    if sw<=0 or not finite: 
       w = v
       sw = v.sum()
    v_new = w/sw
    # optional floor
    if v_new.min() <= 1e-300:
       v_new = v_new*(1-1e-9) + 1e-9/n
       v_new /= v_new.sum()
    C = conv_fft(v_new)
    Dcur = C.max()
    if Dcur < bestD*(1-1e-9):
       bestD=Dcur; best=v_new.copy(); no_improve=0; print occasionally
    else:
       no_improve +=1
       # if no improve for 500 iters, try larger T or random reheat
       if no_improve % 1000 ==0:
           # reheat: multiply by noise
           noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n))
           v_noisy = v*noise; v_noisy/=sum
           C_noisy=conv; if <bestD? We can accept even if slight worse to escape
           score_noisy=2*n*C_noisy.max()
           if score_noisy < score_best*(1+0.02): v=v_noisy; C=C_noisy
    v = v_new
    iters_done +=1
- return best

Let's detail each.

`_normalize(v, total=1.0)`:
```
v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
s = v.sum()
if s <= 1e-300:
    v = np.full(v.size, total/v.size)
else:
    v *= total/s
return v
```
If v size 0, return empty? Not needed.

`_conv_fft(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
if n==0: return np.array([])
size=1
while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
# remove numerical negatives? max can be affected by tiny neg no; for softmax, negative could lower exp; clip small negative to 0.
b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
return b
```
Clipping negatives may slightly alter sum but okay.

`_cross_corr(p,a)`:

out=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fp)*fa, size)[:n]

return out
```

`lp_refine(f0,n,deadline,rng)`:
```

  U_factor = ...
  g = _lp_single(f,n,U_factor)
  if g is None: break
  g = _normalize(g)
  # line search
  best_t=0; best_cand=f; best_cscore=best_score_current
  for t in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
    cand=(1-t)*f + t*g; cand=_normalize(cand)
    sc=score
    if sc<best_cscore: choose
  if best_t>0:
    f=best_cand; if sc<best_score update best
    stuck=0
  else:
    stuck +=1
    if stuck>5:
      # random reheat
      f = normalize(f * exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n)))
      # update best? no
    if stuck>20: break
  it+=1
return best
```

 # Coarse LP/scipy phase (if HAVE_SCIPY):

   coarse_inits = top coarse parametric (maybe same params at m=512)

      c2 = _scipy_optimize(ramp512, 512, time+30, rng)

 for idx, cand in enumerate(candidates_to_refine[:2]):

Function signature: `_mirror_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng)`.
Inside:
```
v = _normalize(v0.copy())
C = _conv_fft(v)
best = v.copy(); bestD=C.max(); bestScore=2*n*bestD
score=2*n*C.max()
phases = [...]
phase_idx=0; it=0; total_it=0; reject_streak=0; T_scale=1.0
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
  if phase_idx >= len(phases): phase_idx = len(phases)-1
  tau, T0, T1 = phases[phase_idx]
  # phase iters target maybe 800
  target = 800 if phase_idx<5 else 1500
  local_it=0
  while time.monotonic()<deadline and local_it<target:
    beta = max(1.0, tau*n)  # tau maybe >=0.1
    # compute p and D
    Cmax=float(C.max())
    z=beta*(C-Cmax)
    z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)  # since C-Cmax<=0 maybe; numerical maybe >0 small clip to 0? if >0, allow small
    expz=np.exp(z)
    s=expz.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: p=np.full(len(C),1/len(C))
    else: p=expz/s
    K=_cross_corr(p,v)
    D=2*K
    dmean=float(D.mean())
    if dmean<=0 or not finite: break
    frac=local_it/max(target,1)
    T = T0 * (T1/T0)**frac * T_scale
    if T < 1e-4: T=1e-4
    zup = -T*(D/dmean - 1.0)
    zup = zup - zup.mean()
    zup = np.clip(zup, -50.0, 50.0)
    w = v * np.exp(zup)
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw<=0 or not finite: w=v; sw=1
    v_new = w/sw
    # floor
    if v_new.min() < 1e-300:
       v_new = v_new*(1-1e-8) + 1e-8/n
       v_new /= v_new.sum()
    C_new = _conv_fft(v_new)
    Dnew=max(C_new)
    score_new=2*n*Dnew
    # soft values
    soft_old = _soft_value(C, beta)
    soft_new = _soft_value(C_new, beta)
    accept = (soft_new <= soft_old + 1e-12) or (score_new < score)
    if not accept and score_new < score*1.005 and rng.random() < 0.3:
       accept = True
    if accept:
       v=v_new; C=C_new; score=score_new
       D=???  # not needed
       reject_streak=0
       T_scale=1.0
       if score < bestScore:
           bestScore=score; best=v.copy(); print...
    else:
       reject_streak +=1
       # attempt reheat if long reject
       if reject_streak % 200 == 0:
           if reject_streak < 800:
               T_scale *= 0.7
           else:
               T_scale *= 1.5
               # maybe random perturbation
               noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
               vp = v*noise; vp/=vp.sum(); Cp=_conv_fft(vp); sp=2*n*Cp.max()
               if sp < bestScore*1.02:
                   v=vp; C=Cp; score=sp
           reject_streak=0
           if T_scale < 0.05: T_scale=0.05
           if T_scale > 10: T_scale=10
       # If many rejects, break phase? Could move to next tau

def _soft_value(C,beta):

`_resample_linear(v, n)`:
```
m=len(v)
if m==n: return normalize(v)
if m==1: return full
if n==1: return [v.sum()]
x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
idx = x * m
i0 = np.floor(idx).astype(int)
i1 = np.minimum(i0+1, m-1)
frac = idx - i0
out = v[i0]*(1-frac) + v[i1]*frac
return normalize(out)
```

`_random_smooth`:
```
def _random_smooth(n, rng):
    x = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(x)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    sigma = max(1.0, n/20.0)
    mask = np.exp(-0.5*((freq*n)/sigma)**2)  # freq cycles/sample; freq*n cycles total
    y = np.fft.irfft(f*mask, n)
    # make positive: y = y - y.min() + 1? Or absolute? If subtract min and add small
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    if y.sum() <= 1e-6:
        y = np.abs(y) + 1e-3
    return y
```
Then normalize in candidate builder. This yields smooth nonnegative. Could find better.

Now, implement `_soft_value` robust:
```
def _soft_value(C, beta):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    if C.size ==0: return 0.0
    cmax = float(np.max(C))
    z = beta * (C - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s): return cmax
    return cmax + float(np.log(s))/beta
```
If beta <=0, return cmax.

`_mirror_refine` uses `beta = max(1.0, tau * n)`. If tau=0.5,n=6733 beta=3366, C~1e-4, beta*C~0.33. Good. For tau=800 beta=5.3M, beta*C~5, good.

```
phases = ((0.5,1.0,0.25), (1.0,0.7,0.2), ...)
```
Inside function local variable okay.

Could hurt convergence. Use reheat only if new score < best_score*1.05 and random. If not, don't change v but maybe T_scale.

Simpler mirror: unconditional accept if soft_new <= soft_old or score_new < score. If reject many, adjust T_scale. Good.

Pseudo code more concrete:
```
def _mirror_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng):
    v = _normalize(v0.copy())
    C = _conv_fft(v)
    score = 2*n*float(C.max())
    best = v.copy(); best_score = score
    no_improve = 0
    phase_idx=0
    T_scale=1.0
    phases = (...)
    total_iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if phase_idx >= len(phases): phase_idx = len(phases)-1
        tau,T0,T1 = phases[phase_idx]
        target = 700 if phase_idx < 6 else 1200
        local_it=0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline and local_it < target:
            beta = max(1.0, tau*n)
            cmax = float(C.max())
            z = beta*(C-cmax)
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
            expz = np.exp(z)
            s = float(expz.sum())
            if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                p = np.full(C.size, 1.0/C.size)
            else:
                p = expz / s
            K = _cross_corr(p, v)
            D = 2.0*K
            dmean = float(D.mean())
            if dmean <=0 or not np.isfinite(dmean):
                break
            frac = local_it / max(target,1)
            T = T0 * (T1 / T0) ** frac * T_scale
            if T < 1e-4: T = 1e-4
            zu = -T * (D / dmean - 1.0)
            zu -= zu.mean()
            zu = np.clip(zu, -50.0, 50.0)
            w = v * np.exp(zu)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if sw <=0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                local_it +=1; continue
            v_new = w / sw
            if v_new.min() <= 1e-300:
                v_new = v_new*(1-1e-8) + 1e-8/n
                v_new /= v_new.sum()
            C_new = _conv_fft(v_new)
            score_new = 2*n*float(C_new.max())
            soft_old = float(cmax + np.log(s)/beta) if s>0 else cmax
            soft_new = _soft_value(C_new, beta)
            accept = (soft_new <= soft_old + 1e-12) or (score_new < score * (1 - 1e-12))
            if not accept and score_new < score * (1 + 0.003) and rng.random() < 0.2:
                accept=True
            if accept:
                v=v_new; C=C_new; score=score_new
                T_scale = 1.0
                if score < best_score * (1 - 1e-10):
                    best_score=score; best=v.copy(); no_improve=0
                    print(...)
                else:
                    no_improve += 10
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve % 200 == 0:
                    if T_scale > 0.2:
                        T_scale *= 0.7
                    else:
                        T_scale *= 1.3
                        # random small reheat
                        noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n))
                        v_trial = v * noise
                        v_trial /= v_trial.sum()
                        C_trial = _conv_fft(v_trial)
                        sc_trial = 2*n*float(C_trial.max())
                        if sc_trial < best_score * 1.01:
                            v=v_trial; C=C_trial; score=sc_trial
            if no_improve > 8000:
                # bigger random restart around best
                v = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)))
                C = _conv_fft(v)
                score = 2*n*float(C.max())
                no_improve = 0
                T_scale = 1.0
            local_it +=1
            total_iter +=1
        if phase_idx < len(phases)-1:
            phase_idx +=1
        # if no_improve huge and near end, break? loop handles
    return best, best_score
```
This returns tuple. In main update.

Potential issue: `print` too frequent if many improvements. Use `if int(total_iter) % 10000 ==0`. But total_iter update after accept? okay.

```
def _local_refine(v0,n,deadline,rng):
 v=normalize; C=conv; score...
 best...
 it=0
 while time<deadline:
   it+=1
   r=rng.random()
   if r<0.4:
     # peak soft step
     cmax=C.max(); beta=max(1.0, 100.0*n)
     z=beta*(C-cmax); expz; p
     K=cross; D=2*K; dmean
     eps = 0.1 * (0.5 + rng.random())
     zu=-eps*(D/dmean-1); zu-=mean; zu=clip; cand=v*exp(zu); normalize; Cc=conv; sc...
   elif r<0.7:
     # block
     width=int(max(4, n*rng.uniform(0.001,0.01)))
     start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
     cand=v.copy(); cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.08,width))
     cand=normalize(cand); Cc=conv; sc
   else:
     cand=v*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.02,n)); normalize...
   if sc < score*(1-1e-12): # accept to current if improves current
       v=cand; C=Cc; score=sc
       if sc < best_score: best...
   elif sc < best_score*(1-1e-10): # improve best not current? accept anyway
       v=cand; C=Cc; score=sc; best...
   # else reject
   if it % 5000 ==0: maybe reheat if no improve
 return best,score
```

`_mf`:
```
def _mf(v, total=1.0):
    v = np.array(v, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if v.ndim !=1:
        v = v.ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(v.size, total/v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return v * (total/s)
```
If total=1 and v.size 0, division by zero; handle before? if size==0 return empty.

`_conv`:
```
def _conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size < need: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:need]
    # numerical cleanup
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```

`_xcorr`:
```
def _xcorr(p,a):
    n=a.size; L=p.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
    size=1
    need=L+n-1
    while size < need: size <<=1
    pr=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(pr*fa, size)[:need]
    start=L-n
    if start <0: start=0
    stop=L
    if stop > need: stop=need
    out=conv[start:stop][::-1]
    # If out length < n due weird, pad
    if out.size < n:
       out=np.pad(out, (0,n-out.size), constant_values=0.0)
    else:
       out=out[:n]
    out=np.where(out>0.0,out,0.0)
    return out
```
For L=2n-1, start=n-1. Good.

`_soft_value` as above.

`_score`:
```
def _score(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size==0: return np.inf
    s=float(np.sum(v))
    if s <=1e-6: return np.inf
    c=_conv(v)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*v.size*float(c.max())/(s*s))
```
If we score normalized many times, s=1.

`_random_smooth`:
```
def _random_smooth(n,rng):
    x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    if n <3: return np.ones(n)
    f=np.fft.rfft(x)
    fr=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    sigma=max(1.0, n/20.0)
    mask=np.exp(-0.5*((fr*n)/sigma)**2)
    y=np.fft.irfft(f*mask, n)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s <=1e-6:
       y=np.abs(y)+1e-3
    return y
```

`_make_top`:
```
def _make_top(n,rng,parent,ntop=4):
    arrays=[]; scores=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    eps=1.0/(n+1)
    xe=x+eps
    oe=1.0-x+eps
    alphas=(0.4,0.6,0.8,1.0,1.3,1.6,2.0)
    betas=(0.0,0.12,0.25,0.5,0.8,1.2)
    for a in alphas:
       ax = xe**a
       ay = oe**a
       for b in betas:
           bx = oe**b # for v=xe^a*oe^b
           v=ax*bx
           if v.sum()>0:
              v=_mf(v); sc=_score(v); arrays.append(v); scores.append(sc)
           by = xe**b
           v=ay*by
           if v.sum()>0: ...
    # ramp
    v=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64); append
    v=v[::-1]; append
    # sine mod
    for amp in (0.3,0.6):
       base=xe
       for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
          v=base*(1.0+amp*np.sign? no sign variable) # use (1+amp*sin), (1-amp*sin)
    # random smooth 12
    for _ in range(12):
       v=_random_smooth(n,rng); if sum>0 append
    # parent
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       # if parent length not n, resample
       if parent.size == n: pv=parent
       else: pv=_resample(parent,n)
       pv=_mf(pv); if sum>0 append
    pairs=[]
    for i in range(len(scores)): pairs.append((float(scores[i]), i))
    pairs.sort()
    top=[]
    for sc,i in pairs[:ntop]: top.append(arrays[i])
    return top
```
No lambda. For sine:
```
base = xe / xe.sum()? normalize later
sinv = np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
   v = np.maximum(base * (1.0 + amp*sinv*sign), 1e-6)
```
Append.

`_lp_single`:
```
def _lp_single(f,U):
    if not _HAVE_SC: return None
    f=_mf(f)
    n=f.size
    c=_conv(f); M=float(c.max())
    if M <=0: return None
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = f
    b=np.full(2*n-1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    cvec=-np.ones(n)
    bounds=[(0.0, U)]*n
    try:
       res=_linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       try: res=_linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds)
       except Exception: return None
    if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False):
       # if status 0?
       if hasattr(res,'status') and res.status==0:
           x=res.x
       else: return None
    else: x=res.x
    if x is None: return None
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).clip(min=0.0)
    if x.sum() <=1e-8: return None
    return x
```
Highs res.success True if status 0. Good.

LP A construction for n=512 each call loops 512; okay. But note for j, `A[j:j+n, j] = f` uses f length n. For j=0 rows0..n-1; for j=n-1 rows n-1..2n-2. Rows before/after zero. This matches (f*g)_k = Σ f_{k-j} g_j. Good.

`_lp_refine`:
Need use `_score` many times. For n=512 line search 6 times*LPs. okay.
```
def _lp_refine(v0,n,deadline,rng):
    v=_mf(v0.copy())
    best=v.copy(); best_score=_score(v); score=best_score
    it=0; stuck=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and stuck<40:
       Ufactors=(3.0,5.0,8.0,12.0)
       uf=Ufactors[it % len(Ufactors)]
       U=max(uf/n, 2.0*float(v.max()) + 1e-12)
       g=_lp_single(v,U)
       if g is None:
          stuck +=1; it+=1; continue
       g=_mf(g)
       mean=1.0/n
       if float(g.max()) > 4.0*mean:
           g = 0.65*g + 0.35*np.full(n, mean)
           g=_mf(g)
       chosen=None; chosen_score=score
       for t in (0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
           cand=(1.0-t)*v + t*g
           cand=_mf(cand)
           sc=_score(cand)
           if sc < chosen_score:
               chosen=cand; chosen_score=sc
       if chosen is not None and chosen_score < score*(1-1e-12):
           v=chosen; score=chosen_score; stuck=0
           if score < best_score:
               best_score=score; best=v.copy(); print...
       else:
           stuck +=1
           if stuck % 5 ==0:
               noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n))
               v=_mf(v*noise)
               score=_score(v)
               # do not change best
       it+=1
    return best, best_score
```
If v random reheat worse, score worsening but current v moved; okay. If score too bad, line search next may recover. Could keep best separately.

`_smooth_fun_w`:
```
def _smooth_fun_w(w,n,beta):
    z=w - np.max(w)
    e=np.exp(-np.abs(z))? Wait stable softmax: w may large. Use z=w-w.max; e=exp(z); v=e/sum.
    if sum<=0: v=ones(n)/n
    C=_conv(v)
    cmax=C.max()
    zz=beta*(C-cmax); zz=np.clip(zz,-50,0)
    p=np.exp(zz); s=p.sum()
    fval = cmax + np.log(s)/beta (maybe scaled 2n? use cmax only)
    if s<=0: p=np.full(C.size,1/C.size)
    else p/=s
    K=_xcorr(p,v)
    D=2*K
    dot=float(np.dot(v,D))
    grad=D - v*dot
    # return 2n*fval? constant; use 2n*fval to scale
    return float(2.0*n*fval), grad
```
For L-BFGS, gradient same scale if multiply f by 2n. Good.
If beta <=0, grad? beta always positive.

`_scipy_refine`:
```
def _scipy_refine(v0,n,deadline,rng):
    if not _HAVE_SC: return v0.copy(), _score(v0)
    v=_mf(v0.copy()); best=v.copy(); best_score=_score(v)
    w=np.log(v + 1e-300)
    tau=0.5
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and tau < 256.0:
       beta=max(1.0, tau*n)
       try:
           res=_minimize(_smooth_fun_w, w, args=(n,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                         options={'maxiter':700,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':50,'disp':False})
           if res is not None and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
               v2=_mf(_softmax_from_log(res.x)) # need helper without nested
               sc=_score(v2)
               if sc < best_score:
                  best_score=sc; best=v2.copy()
               w=res.x
       except Exception:
           break
       tau *= 2.0
    return best,best_score
```
Need `_softmax_from_log` top-level:
```
def _softmax_log(w):
    w=np.asarray(w)
    z=w-w.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full(w.size,1/w.size)
    return e/s
```
Use in `_smooth_fun_w` too.

`_smooth_fun_w` uses `_softmax_log`.

`_scipy_refine` for n=512 maybe 8 beta*700=5600 eval; could be >minutes? Each eval small but Python overhead maybe. Limit maxiter 200 for coarse. Use `maxiter=300`. We can also stop when time. L-BFGS may run maxiter anyway. Use 200.

Now, main `propose_candidate` details with time allocation.

```
def propose_candidate():
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 1490.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234)
    par = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if par is not None:
       try:
          par=np.asarray(par,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          par=np.where(np.isfinite(par),par,0.0); par=np.maximum(par,0.0)
          if par.size==0: par=None
       except: par=None
    if par is not None and 1500 <= par.size <= 20000:
       N=int(par.size)
    else:
       N=4096
    top = _make_top(N,rng,par,5)
    if not top:
       v = np.arange(1,N+1,dtype=np.float64); v=_mf(v); top=[v]
    best = _mf(top[0]); best_score=_score(best)
    print('initial', best_score, N)
    now=time.monotonic()
    if _HAVE_SC:
       coarse_N=512
       # LP phase for two coarse candidates up to 50s each or until deadline-500
       if now < deadline - 500:
           ctop = _make_top(coarse_N, rng, None, 3)
           for idx, cv in enumerate(ctop[:2]):
               t_dead = min(deadline - 400, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
               if time.monotonic() >= t_dead: break
               cv_best, cv_sc = _lp_refine(cv, coarse_N, t_dead, rng)
               up = _resample(cv_best, N)
               sc = _score(up)
               print('coarse LP', idx, cv_sc, 'up', sc)
               if sc < best_score:
                   best=up; best_score=sc
           # scipy fine
           t_dead = min(deadline - 300, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
           if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
               ctop2 = _make_top(coarse_N, rng, None, 1)
               if ctop2:
                   cv_best, cv_sc = _scipy_refine(ctop2[0], coarse_N, t_dead, rng)
                   up=_resample(cv_best,N); sc=_score(up)
                   if sc<best_score: best=up; best_score=sc
    # Mirror top candidate
    t_dead = min(deadline - 250, time.monotonic() + 250.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
       m_best, m_sc = _mirror_refine(best, N, t_dead, rng)
       if m_sc < best_score: best=m_best; best_score=m_sc
       print('mirror1', best_score)
    # Alternative
    if len(top) > 1:
       alt = _mf(top[1])
       if _score(alt) < 2.5: # maybe
          t_dead = min(deadline - 150, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
          if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
              m2,m2s=_mirror_refine(alt,N,t_dead,rng)
              if m2s<best_score: best=m2; best_score=m2s
    # Long mirror
    t_dead = min(deadline - 60, time.monotonic() + 400.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
       m_best,m_sc=_mirror_refine(best,N,t_dead,rng)
       if m_sc<best_score: best=m_best; best_score=m_sc
    # local
    t_dead = min(deadline - 20, time.monotonic() + 200.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
       l_best,l_sc=_local_refine(best,N,t_dead,rng)
       if l_sc<best_score: best=l_best; best_score=l_sc
    best = _mf(best)
    # verify with evaluate_sequence on best and maybe a couple
    candidates = [best]
    if len(top)>0: candidates.append(top[0])
    if len(top)>1: candidates.append(top[1])
    # unique? not necessary
    final=best; final_eval=np.inf
    for c in candidates[:3]:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline -5: break
       c=_mf(c)
       try:
          ev=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in c]))
       except Exception:
          ev=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_eval:
          final_eval=ev; final=c
    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
       return [float(x) for x in final]
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

Double-check `_smooth_fun_w`: It uses `_softmax_log(w)`. If n=0? not.
```
def _smooth_fun_w(w, n, beta):
    v = _softmax_log(w)
    C = _conv(v)
    cmax = float(C.max())
    z = beta * (C - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(C.size, 1.0 / max(C.size,1))
        fval = cmax
    else:
        p = e / s
        fval = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta
    K = _xcorr(p, v)
    D = 2.0 * K
    dot = float(np.dot(v, D))
    grad = D - v * dot
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad = np.zeros(n)
    return float(2.0 * n * fval), grad
```
If `grade` not finite? fine.

`_softmax_log`:
```
def _softmax_log(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size==0: return np.array([])
    z=w-np.max(w)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return full
    return e/s
```

def _local_refine(v0,n,deadline,rng):
 v=_mf(v0); C=_conv(v); score=...
 best=v; best_score=score; no_improve=0; it=0
 while time<deadline:
  it+=1
  r=float(rng.random())
  cand=None; Cc=None; sc=np.inf
  if r <0.4:
     cmax=C.max(); beta=max(1.0, 100.0*n)
     z=beta*(C-cmax); z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
     if s>0: p=e/s; K=_xcorr(p,v); D=2*K; dm=D.mean();
     if dm>0:
       eps=0.05+0.15*float(rng.random())
       zu=-eps*(D/dm-1); zu-=zu.mean(); zu=np.clip(zu,-30,30)
       cand=_mf(v*np.exp(zu)); Cc=_conv(cand); sc=...
  elif r<0.7:
     width=max(4,int(n*float(rng.uniform(0.001,0.01))))
     start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width+1)))
     cand=v.copy(); cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.08,width))
     cand=_mf(cand); Cc=_conv(cand); sc=...
  else:
     cand=_mf(v*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.02,n))); Cc=_conv(cand); sc=...
  if cand is not None and sc < score*(1-1e-12):
     v=cand; C=Cc; score=sc
     if sc < best_score: best=v.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
     else: no_improve+=1
  elif cand is not None and sc < best_score*(1-1e-10):
     v=cand; C=Cc; score=sc; best=v.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
  else:
     no_improve +=1
  if no_improve > 2000:
     noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n))
     vp=_mf(v*noise); Cp=_conv(vp); sp=...
     if sp < best_score*1.01: v=vp; C=Cp; score=sp
     no_improve=0
  if no_improve > 10000: break
 return best,best_score
```

```python
"""Convolution minimizer using parametric starts, coarse LP/smooth optimization,
mirror-descent refinement, and local multiplicative search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _normalize(v, total=1.0):
    """Project an array to a non-negative vector with the requested sum."""
    v = np.array(v, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if v.ndim != 1:
        v = v.ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(v.size, total / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return v * (float(total) / s)


def _conv(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:need]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c


def _xcorr(p, a):
    """
    Compute K_k = sum_m p_m * a_{m-k}, the needed gradient term.
    p has length 2n-1, a has length n.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = L + n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(pr * fa, size)[:need]
    start = L - n
    if start < 0:
        start = 0
    stop = L
    if stop > need:
        stop = need
    out = conv[start:stop][::-1]
    if out.size < n:
        out = np.pad(out, (0, n - out.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        out = out[:n]
    out = np.where(out > 0.0, out, 0.0)
    return out


def _soft_value(C, beta):
    """Stable log-sum-exp approximation to max(C)."""
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    if C.size == 0 or beta <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    cmax = float(np.max(C))
    z = beta * (C - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return cmax
    return cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta


def _score(v):
    """Exact objective for a finite non-negative sequence."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-6:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv(v)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    out = float(2.0 * v.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))
    if not np.isfinite(out):
        return np.inf
    return out


def _softmax_log(w):
    """Stable softmax."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = w - float(np.max(w))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(w.size, 1.0 / w.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _random_smooth(n, rng):
    """Generate a smooth non-negative random shape."""
    x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if n < 3:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.fft.rfft(x)
    fr = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    sigma = max(1.0, n / 20.0)
    mask = np.exp(-0.5 * ((fr * n) / sigma) ** 2)
    y = np.fft.irfft(f * mask, n)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    if y.sum() <= 1e-6:
        y = np.abs(y) + 1e-3
    return y


def _resample(v, n):
    """Linear resample a normalized vector to length n."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = v.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return _normalize(v)
    if n == 1:
        out = np.array([float(v.sum())], dtype=np.float64)
        return _normalize(out)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    idx = x * float(m)
    i0 = np.floor(idx).astype(np.int64)
    i1 = np.minimum(i0 + 1, m - 1)
    frac = idx - i0.astype(np.float64)
    out = v[i0] * (1.0 - frac) + v[i1] * frac
    return _normalize(out)


def _make_top(n, rng, parent, ntop=4):
    """Create a shortlist of promising normalized initial sequences."""
    n = int(n)
    arrays = []
    scores = []

    def add(v):
        v = _normalize(v)
        if v.size != n or v.sum() <= 1e-6:
            return
        sc = _score(v)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = np.inf
        arrays.append(v)
        scores.append(float(sc))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    eps = 1.0 / (n + 1)
    xe = x + eps
    oe = 1.0 - x + eps

    alphas = (0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.6, 2.0)
    betas = (0.0, 0.12, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2)

    for a in alphas:
        ax = xe ** a
        ay = oe ** a
        for b in betas:
            add(ax * (oe ** b))
            add(ay * (xe ** b))

    v = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    add(v)
    add(v[::-1])

    sinv = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    for amp in (0.3, 0.6):
        add(np.maximum(xe * (1.0 + amp * sinv), 1e-8))
        add(np.maximum(xe * (1.0 - amp * sinv), 1e-8))

    for _ in range(12):
        add(_random_smooth(n, rng))

    if parent is not None:
        pv = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if pv.size == n:
            add(pv)
        else:
            add(_resample(pv, n))

    pairs = []
    for i in range(len(scores)):
        pairs.append((scores[i], i))
    pairs.sort()

    top = []
    for _, idx in pairs[:max(1, int(ntop))]:
        top.append(arrays[idx])
    if not top:
        top.append(_normalize(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)))
    return top


def _lp_single(f, U):
    """One LP correction step from the state-of-art idea."""
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    c = _conv(f)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = f
    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, float(U))] * n

    res = None
    try:
        res = _linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = _linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None:
        return None

    ok = False
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is not None:
        if getattr(res, "success", False):
            ok = True
        elif getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0:
            ok = True
    if not ok:
        return None

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    if x.sum() <= 1e-8:
        return None
    return x


def _lp_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng):
    """Coarse LP-based improvement loop."""
    v = _normalize(v0.copy())
    score = _score(v)
    best = v.copy()
    best_score = score
    it = 0
    stuck = 0
    ufactors = (3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 12.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and stuck < 40:
        uf = ufactors[it % len(ufactors)]
        U = max(uf / float(n), 2.0 * float(v.max()) + 1e-12)
        g = _lp_single(v, U)
        if g is None:
            stuck += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        g = _normalize(g)
        mean = 1.0 / float(n)
        if float(g.max()) > 4.0 * mean:
            g = 0.65 * g + 0.35 * np.full(n, mean, dtype=np.float64)
            g = _normalize(g)

        chosen = None
        chosen_score = score
        for t in (0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * v + t * g
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            sc = _score(cand)
            if sc < chosen_score:
                chosen = cand
                chosen_score = sc

        if chosen is not None and chosen_score < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            v = chosen
            score = chosen_score
            stuck = 0
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best = v.copy()
                if it % 10 == 0:
                    print("LP coarse improve", float(score))
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck % 5 == 0:
                v = _normalize(v * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)))
                score = _score(v)
        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def _smooth_fun_w(w, n, beta):
    """Soft-maximized convolution objective on the log-sum-exp simplex."""
    v = _softmax_log(w)
    C = _conv(v)
    cmax = float(C.max())
    z = beta * (C - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(C.size, 1.0 / max(C.size, 1), dtype=np.float64)
        fval = cmax
    else:
        p = e / s
        fval = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    K = _xcorr(p, v)
    D = 2.0 * K
    dot = float(np.dot(v, D))
    grad = D - v * dot
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(2.0 * n * fval), grad


def _scipy_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng):
    """Local smooth optimization of a coarse shape."""
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return _normalize(v0.copy()), _score(v0)

    v = _normalize(v0.copy())
    best = v.copy()
    best_score = _score(v)
    w = np.log(v + 1e-300)
    tau = 0.5

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and tau < 256.0:
        beta = max(1.0, tau * float(n))
        try:
            res = _minimize(
                _smooth_fun_w,
                w,
                args=(n, beta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 300,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-9,
                    "maxls": 50,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                v2 = _normalize(_softmax_log(res.x))
                sc = _score(v2)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best = v2.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                w = res.x
        except Exception:
            break
        tau *= 2.0

    return best, best_score


def _mirror_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng):
    """Annealed mirror-descent refinement of the smoothed maximum."""
    v = _normalize(v0.copy())
    C = _conv(v)
    score = float(2.0 * n * C.max())
    best = v.copy()
    best_score = score
    no_improve = 0
    phase_idx = 0
    T_scale = 1.0
    last_print = 0.0

    phases = (
        (0.5, 1.00, 0.25),
        (1.0, 0.70, 0.20),
        (2.0, 0.50, 0.16),
        (5.0, 0.35, 0.12),
        (10.0, 0.25, 0.09),
        (20.0, 0.18, 0.07),
        (50.0, 0.12, 0.05),
        (100.0, 0.08, 0.035),
        (200.0, 0.05, 0.02),
        (400.0, 0.03, 0.012),
        (800.0, 0.02, 0.006),
    )

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if phase_idx >= len(phases):
            phase_idx = len(phases) - 1
        tau, T0, T1 = phases[phase_idx]
        target = 800 if phase_idx < 6 else 1500
        local_it = 0

        while time.monotonic() < deadline and local_it < target:
            beta = max(1.0, tau * float(n))
            cmax = float(C.max())
            z = beta * (C - cmax)
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(z)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                p = np.full(C.size, 1.0 / C.size, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                p = e / s

            K = _xcorr(p, v)
            D = 2.0 * K
            dmean = float(D.mean())
            if dmean <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(dmean):
                break

            frac = local_it / float(max(target, 1))
            T = T0 * (T1 / T0) ** frac * T_scale
            if T < 1e-4:
                T = 1e-4

            zu = -T * (D / dmean - 1.0)
            zu = zu - float(zu.mean())
            zu = np.clip(zu, -50.0, 50.0)
            w = v * np.exp(zu)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
                local_it += 1
                continue

            v_new = w / sw
            if float(v_new.min()) <= 1e-300:
                v_new = v_new * (1.0 - 1e-8) + 1e-8 / float(n)
                v_new = _normalize(v_new)

            C_new = _conv(v_new)
            score_new = float(2.0 * n * C_new.max())
            soft_old = cmax + (float(np.log(s)) / beta if s > 0.0 else 0.0)
            soft_new = _soft_value(C_new, beta)

            accept = (soft_new <= soft_old + 1e-12) or (score_new < score * (1.0 - 1e-12))
            if not accept and score_new < score * (1.003) and float(rng.random()) < 0.2:
                accept = True

            if accept:
                v = v_new
                C = C_new
                score = score_new
                T_scale = 1.0
                if score < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                    best_score = score
                    best = v.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    if time.monotonic() - last_print > 15.0:
                        last_print = time.monotonic()
                        print("mirror improve", float(best_score))
                else:
                    no_improve += 5
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve % 200 == 0:
                    if T_scale > 0.2:
                        T_scale *= 0.7
                    else:
                        T_scale *= 1.3
                        vp = _normalize(v * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)))
                        Cp = _conv(vp)
                        sp = float(2.0 * n * Cp.max())
                        if sp < best_score * 1.01:
                            v = vp
                            C = Cp
                            score = sp
                    if T_scale < 0.05:
                        T_scale = 0.05
                    if T_scale > 10.0:
                        T_scale = 10.0

            if no_improve > 8000:
                v = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)))
                C = _conv(v)
                score = float(2.0 * n * C.max())
                no_improve = 0
                T_scale = 1.0

            local_it += 1

        if phase_idx < len(phases) - 1:
            phase_idx += 1

    return best, best_score


def _local_refine(v0, n, deadline, rng):
    """Final multiplicative local search with peak-aware moves."""
    v = _normalize(v0.copy())
    C = _conv(v)
    score = float(2.0 * n * C.max())
    best = v.copy()
    best_score = score
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        r = float(rng.random())
        cand = None
        Cc = None
        sc = np.inf

        if r < 0.40:
            cmax = float(C.max())
            beta = max(1.0, 100.0 * float(n))
            z = beta * (C - cmax)
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(z)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                p = e / s
                K = _xcorr(p, v)
                D = 2.0 * K
                dm = float(D.mean())
                if dm > 0.0 and np.isfinite(dm):
                    eps = 0.05 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
                    zu = -eps * (D / dm - 1.0)
                    zu = zu - float(zu.mean())
                    zu = np.clip(zu, -30.0, 30.0)
                    cand = _normalize(v * np.exp(zu))
                    Cc = _conv(cand)
                    sc = float(2.0 * n * Cc.max())
        elif r < 0.70:
            width = max(4, int(float(n) * float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.01))))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            cand = v.copy()
            cand[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, width))
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            Cc = _conv(cand)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * Cc.max())
        else:
            cand = _normalize(v * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)))
            Cc = _conv(cand)
            sc = float(2.0 * n * Cc.max())

        if cand is not None:
            if sc < score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                v = cand
                C = Cc
                score = sc
                if sc < best_score:
                    best = v.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            elif sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                v = cand
                C = Cc
                score = sc
                best = v.copy()
                best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 2000:
            vp = _normalize(v * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)))
            Cp = _conv(vp)
            sp = float(2.0 * n * Cp.max())
            if sp < best_score * 1.01:
                v = vp
                C = Cp
                score = sp
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 10000:
            break

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """Run the full search and return the best non-negative sequence found."""
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(60.0, budget - 15.0)

    parent = None
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if par is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
            par = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
            if par.size > 0:
                parent = par
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None and 1500 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        N = int(parent.size)
    else:
        N = 4096

    top = _make_top(N, rng, parent, 5)
    best = _normalize(top[0])
    best_score = _score(best)
    print("initial best", float(best_score), "n", N)

    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
        coarse_N = 256
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500:
            ctop = _make_top(coarse_N, rng, None, 3)
            for idx, cv in enumerate(ctop[:2]):
                t_dead = min(deadline - 400, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
                if time.monotonic() >= t_dead:
                    break
                cv_best, cv_sc = _lp_refine(cv, coarse_N, t_dead, rng)
                up = _resample(cv_best, N)
                sc = _score(up)
                print("coarse LP", idx, float(cv_sc), "upsampled", float(sc))
                if sc < best_score:
                    best = up
                    best_score = sc

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300:
                t_dead = min(deadline - 250, time.monotonic() + 45.0)
                ctop2 = _make_top(coarse_N, rng, None, 1)
                if ctop2:
                    cv_best, cv_sc = _scipy_refine(ctop2[0], coarse_N, t_dead, rng)
                    up = _resample(cv_best, N)
                    sc = _score(up)
                    print("coarse scipy", float(cv_sc), "upsampled", float(sc))
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = up
                        best_score = sc

    t_dead = min(deadline - 250, time.monotonic() + 250.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
        mb, msc = _mirror_refine(best, N, t_dead, rng)
        if msc < best_score:
            best = mb
            best_score = msc
        print("mirror stage 1", float(best_score))

    if len(top) > 1:
        alt = _normalize(top[1])
        if _score(alt) < 2.5:
            t_dead = min(deadline - 150, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
            if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
                mb, msc = _mirror_refine(alt, N, t_dead, rng)
                if msc < best_score:
                    best = mb
                    best_score = msc
                print("mirror stage alt", float(best_score))

    t_dead = min(deadline - 60, time.monotonic() + 400.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
        mb, msc = _mirror_refine(best, N, t_dead, rng)
        if msc < best_score:
            best = mb
            best_score = msc
        print("mirror stage long", float(best_score))

    t_dead = min(deadline - 25, time.monotonic() + 220.0)
    if time.monotonic() < t_dead:
        lb, lsc = _local_refine(best, N, t_dead, rng)
        if lsc < best_score:
            best = lb
            best_score = lsc
        print("local", float(best_score))

    best = _normalize(best)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
        try:
            ev = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best]))
            if np.isfinite(ev):
                print("final verified eval", ev)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
